**ASCII only.** Living residual Open Names (plain English): see
`RESIDUAL-systems.md`. **Self-host body** is defined (SELF-HOST-BODY). Track 3
**B** freestanding kernel rebuild evidence is **partial**; product kernel
self-application is **partial B2**; product Out kernel evidence is **partial B3**
(Out-first B-bar APIs on `out/freestanding-c/`); product path Lake bootstrap
honesty is **partial B4** (host Lake still writes product wire); product writer
surface substrate is **partial B5** (plan/apply/body + B-bar on product Out);
product writer path **plan** substrate is **partial B6** (ordered freestanding-
capable regenerate steps named); product writer path **execution evidence** is
**partial B7** (host FreestandingEmit + just build perform plan
steps); freestanding product path **ownership inputs** is **partial B8**
(Mult..Out SSOT / emit / Out surfaces named; ownership claimed false); product
path **perform gap** is **partial B9** (Lake still performs read/compose/write;
install step mechanism cp capable; freestanding perform claimed false); first
**perform step** is **partial B10** (Lake-free install entrypoint
`just install-freestanding-c-out`; install entrypoint claimed true; perform
claimed false); **READ-SSOT substrate** is **partial B11** (decomposed dual SSOT
read via ProductPathReadSsot / `just read-product-ssot`; ReadDependsOnLake true;
perform claimed false); **COMPOSE substrate** is **partial B12** (decomposed
plan/apply/body compose via ProductPathComposePlan / `just compose-product-plan`;
ComposeDependsOnLake true; perform claimed false); **WRITE-HC substrate** is
**partial B13** (decomposed freestanding .h/.c write via ProductPathWriteHc /
`just write-freestanding-hc` calling FreestandingEmit.emitAtRoot;
WriteHcDependsOnLake true; perform claimed false); **freestanding-capable gap
measure** is **partial B14 + B26 CapableRead + B27 CapableCompose + B28 CapableWriteHc**
(SELF-HOST-PRODUCT-PATH-FREESTANDING-CAPABLE-GAP: install Lake-free true;
CapableRead true after B26 via `just freestanding-capable-read-lake-free`
token+HEADER/BODY no lake; CapableCompose true after B27 via
`just freestanding-capable-compose-lake-free` plan/apply/body token+HEADER/BODY
no lake; CapableWriteHc true after B28 via `just freestanding-capable-write-hc-lake-free`
dual SSOT token+HEADER/BODY + write substrate no lake; full perform false;
`just regenerate-product-path` ordered host pipeline join; complete flag stays false); **freestanding-capable
step contract** is **partial B15**
(SELF-HOST-PRODUCT-PATH-FREESTANDING-CAPABLE-STEP-CONTRACT: named freestanding-
capable READ/COMPOSE/WRITE-HC contracts + API surface + authority-not-
FreestandingEmit as product path; READ satisfied true after B16; Compose
satisfied true after B17; WriteHc satisfied true after B18; Full false;
complete stays false);
**freestanding-capable READ** is **partial B16 + B26 CapableRead**
(SELF-HOST-PRODUCT-PATH-FREESTANDING-CAPABLE-READ: freestandingCapableReadDualSsot
no FreestandingEmit product authority; HEADER/BODY structural dual SSOT;
ReadSatisfied true; B14 CapableRead true after B26 Lake-free
freestanding-capable-read-lake-free; B16 Lake exe remains diagnostic; complete
stays false);
**freestanding-capable COMPOSE** is **partial B17 + B27 CapableCompose**
(SELF-HOST-PRODUCT-PATH-FREESTANDING-CAPABLE-COMPOSE:
freestandingCapableComposePlanApplyBody no FreestandingEmit product authority;
plan/apply/body HEADER/BODY + ordered marker; ComposeSatisfied true; B14
CapableCompose true after B27 Lake-free freestanding-capable-compose-lake-free;
B17 Lake exe remains diagnostic; complete stays false);
**freestanding-capable WRITE-HC** is **partial B18 + B28 CapableWriteHc**
(SELF-HOST-PRODUCT-PATH-FREESTANDING-CAPABLE-WRITE-HC:
freestandingCapableWriteFreestandingHc no FreestandingEmit product authority;
dual SSOT HEADER/BODY + template embed + emit .h/.c write; WriteHcSatisfied true;
B14 CapableWriteHc true after B28 Lake-free freestanding-capable-write-hc-lake-free;
B18 Lake exe remains diagnostic; complete stays false);
**freestanding-capable ordered regenerate** is **partial B19**
(SELF-HOST-PRODUCT-PATH-FREESTANDING-CAPABLE-REGENERATE:
freestandingCapableOrderedRegenerate READ then COMPOSE then WRITE-HC then Install

Bootstrap is host-assisted. Lake elaborates early Slake drivers. Missing a
freestanding Slake binary is the **starting state** (S0 done = start, not
finish), not a blocker. Do not forge StillUsesLake / DependsOnLake false before
S4 elaborator proof.

| Stage | Name | Status | Honest meaning |
|-------|------|--------|----------------|
| **S0** | Host foundation | **done** | Lake elaborates Systems Lean; product wire residual free; claim B writer-path complete true |
| **S1** | First compiler surface | **done** | Named Mult unit input `SLAKE_FIRST_SURFACE_MULT` -> `firstSurfaceReady` (unitCompileReady + HOST-EMIT-MULT); module `FirstSurface`; `just first-surface` / `slake-first-surface` |
| **S2** | Emit freestanding C for that subset | **done** | Host-built Mult unit package write (`slake_mult_subset.{h,c}`) from S1 Mult input + HOST-EMIT-MULT; `MultSubsetEmit` / `just mult-subset-emit` |
| **S3** | Rebuild own freestanding subset | **done** | Measured Mult subset self-application (`MultSubsetRebuild` / `just mult-subset-rebuild`); re-emit/re-validate S2 package; Lake still allowed; without-Lake finished later |
| **M1** | Linear subset emit (unit surface growth) | **done** | Host-built Linear unit package write (`slake_linear_subset.{h,c}`) from COMPILE-PATH-LINEAR + HOST-EMIT-LINEAR; `LinearSubsetEmit` / `just linear-subset-emit`; Lake remains |

| **S4** | Retire Lake from product path | deferred | DependsOnLake / StillUsesLake false only with elaborator proof -- never forge early |

## Self-host body (SELF-HOST-BODY) -- acceptance first

**Greppable:** SELF-HOST-BODY, HOST-SELF-HOST-BODY, SLAKE_SELF_HOST_BODY_V0,
defined freestanding compile step, SLAKE_EMIT_FREESTANDING_C_V0, HOST-EMIT-SSOT,
HOST-EMIT-MULT, HOST-EMIT-LINEAR, HOST-EMIT-ERASURE, HOST-EMIT-EXTRACT,
HOST-EMIT-TYPES, HOST-EMIT-PROGRAM, HOST-EMIT-GRAPH, HOST-EMIT-COMPOSE,
HOST-EMIT-PLAN, HOST-EMIT-APPLY, HOST-EMIT-BODY, selfHostBodyReady, emitPlanReady, emitApplyReady, emitBodyReady, emitExtractReady

This residual defines the **first real freestanding compile step** with a tiny
input surface and written acceptance. It is **not** another readiness canary
re-list of Mult..Emit / ProductPath / SelfApply.

### Input surface

| Piece | Path / owner |
|-------|----------------|
| Body dialect SSOT | `emit/host_emit_body_fragment.ssot.txt` (HOST-EMIT-SSOT) |
| Mult product text SSOT | `emit/host_emit_mult.ssot.txt` + Lean `EmitMult` (HOST-EMIT-MULT) |
| Linear product text SSOT | `emit/host_emit_linear.ssot.txt` + Lean `EmitLinear` (HOST-EMIT-LINEAR) |
| Erasure product text SSOT | `emit/host_emit_erasure.ssot.txt` + Lean `EmitErasure` (HOST-EMIT-ERASURE) |
| Extract + FAIL_CLOSED product text SSOT | `emit/host_emit_extract.ssot.txt` + Lean `EmitExtract` (HOST-EMIT-EXTRACT) |
| Types product text SSOT | `emit/host_emit_types.ssot.txt` + Lean `EmitTypes` (HOST-EMIT-TYPES) |
| IR program product text SSOT | `emit/host_emit_program.ssot.txt` + Lean `EmitProgram` (HOST-EMIT-PROGRAM) |
| IR graph product text SSOT | `emit/host_emit_graph.ssot.txt` + Lean `EmitGraph` (HOST-EMIT-GRAPH) |
| Host compose product text SSOT | `emit/host_emit_compose.ssot.txt` + Lean `EmitCompose` (HOST-EMIT-COMPOSE) |
| Emit plan product text SSOT | `emit/host_emit_plan.ssot.txt` + Lean `EmitPlan` (HOST-EMIT-PLAN) |
| Emit apply product text SSOT | `emit/host_emit_apply.ssot.txt` + Lean `EmitApply` (HOST-EMIT-APPLY) |
| Emit body scaffolding SSOT | `emit/host_emit_body_fragment.ssot.txt` + Lean `EmitBody` (HOST-EMIT-BODY + HOST-EMIT-SSOT) |
| Wire templates | `emit/template_slake_freestanding.{h,c}.in` |
| Host emit writer | Lake exe `slake-emit-freestanding-c` (`SystemsLean.FreestandingEmit`) |

### Process

1. Lean host loads host SSOT + templates and writes product wire under
   `src/systems/emit/slake_freestanding.{h,c}` (stage `SLAKE_EMIT_FREESTANDING_C_V0`).
2. `just build` copies the release surface to `out/freestanding-c/`.
3. Host pin `SystemsLean/SelfHostBody.lean` states this path and proves
   freestanding product self-host complete and residual free stay **false**.

### Output artifacts

- `src/systems/emit/slake_freestanding.{h,c}` -- product wire (generator output)
- `out/freestanding-c/slake_freestanding.{h,c}` -- release surface copy
- Greppable ownership tokens on the wire: HOST-EMIT-MULT, HOST-EMIT-LINEAR, HOST-EMIT-ERASURE,
  HOST-EMIT-EXTRACT, HOST-EMIT-TYPES, HOST-EMIT-PROGRAM, HOST-EMIT-GRAPH, HOST-EMIT-COMPOSE,
  HOST-EMIT-PLAN, HOST-EMIT-APPLY, HOST-EMIT-SSOT,
  MULT-0/1/OMEGA, LINEAR-EXACT-ONCE, RUNTIME-FS, not residual free

### What green means

| Gate / path | Role |
|-------------|------|
| `just systems-host` | Host module + SELF-HOST-BODY presence tokens |
| `just systems-emit-wire` | Emit product + FreestandingEmit + unit walk |
| `just build` / `./src/systems/check.sh` | End-to-end emit write + release copy + hosted probe |
| Lake smokes on `SelfHostBody` | `selfHostBodyReady_true`; complete/free false proved |

Host pin: `selfHostBodyReady` = `EmitMult.emitMultReady` &&
`EmitLinear.emitLinearReady` && `EmitErasure.emitErasureReady` &&
`EmitExtract.emitExtractReady` && `EmitTypes.emitTypesReady` &&
`EmitProgram.emitProgramReady` && `EmitGraph.emitGraphReady` &&
`EmitCompose.emitComposeReady` && `EmitPlan.emitPlanReady` &&
`EmitApply.emitApplyReady` && surface cites && freestanding emit stage cite &&
!complete && !residual free && !llvm unlock.
Does **not** re-fold ProductPath / SelfApply readiness theater.

| Claim | Status |
|-------|--------|

| residual free (product side) | **true** (2026-08-01 Step 3; DualResidual product residual remains false; host elaborator residual remains) |

just build

## Product residual free measurement (Track 4 / claim A)

**Greppable:** SELF-HOST-RESIDUAL-FREE-MEASURE, PRODUCT-RESIDUAL-FREE-MEASURE,
SLAKE_PRODUCT_RESIDUAL_FREE_MEASURE_V0, residualFreeClaimed,
productResidualRemains, DualResidual, out/freestanding-c

**Status (2026-08-01 Step 3):** product residual free **claimed true**
(`DualResidual.residualFreeClaimed` true; `productResidualRemains` false;
host elaborator residual remains). Measurement path + honesty agreement closed
claim A. Free is **not** host elaborator free, **not** proof complete, **not**
PROVABLY, **not** LLVM unlock, **not** Lake gone.

### What product residual free measures

| Side | Free means (this claim) | Gate surface |
|------|-------------------------|--------------|
| **Product** | No managed Lean runtime / GC / forbidden managed residual markers on the freestanding **release** product wire | pure Nix scan of `out/freestanding-c/slake_freestanding.{h,c}` when present (`systems-emit-wire` PRODUCT-RESIDUAL-FREE-MEASURE) |
| **Host elaborator** | Classic Lake residual gone | **Not** this measure; DualResidual keeps host elaborator residual while Lake hosts |

**Forbidden managed residual (fail if present on release source):** classic Lean
C runtime API / headers (`lean_object`, `lean_inc`, `lean_dec`, `lean_alloc_*`,
`lean_initialize`, `lean/lean.h`, `libleanrt`, ...), Boehm/GC markers
(`GC_malloc`, `GC_gcollect`, `GC_INIT`, `boehm_gc`), GMP residual includes,
`pthread_create` / `pthread_join`, and hosted libc includes (`stdio.h`,
`stdlib.h`, `string.h`, `pthread.h`) that freestanding product wire must not pull
as residual. Full list SSoT: `nix/systems-emit-wire/residual-free-measure.nix`.

**Required freestanding honesty (when release present):**
`SLAKE_EMIT_FREESTANDING_C_V0`, `RUNTIME-FS`, `no product GC`, `product residual free`.

**Out of scope for this measure alone:** nm/link of a freestanding object (text
scan only); selling probe green as free; host elaborator residual free; proof
complete; llvm unlock; PROVABLY. (Claim B complete is a separate true bar.)

### Dual residual honesty (HOST-DUAL-RESIDUAL)

| Pin | Value / rule |
|-----|----------------|
| `hostElaboratorResidualRemains` | **true** (Lake managed residual remains) |
| `productResidualRemains` | **false** (product residual free claimed) |
| `residualFreeClaimed` | **true** (claim A; product side only) |
| `hostElaboratorResidualFreeClaimed` | **false** (do not forge host free) |
| Measure green | Managed residual tokens **absent** on release; required honesty present |

### Flip policy (product residual free)

Flip `residualFreeClaimed` to **true** only when **all** hold (met 2026-08-01):

1. PRODUCT-RESIDUAL-FREE-MEASURE green on release `out/freestanding-c` (forbidden
   managed residual absent; freestanding honesty present).
2. DualResidual deliberately flips product residual free with proved non-claims
   for proof complete / llvm / PROVABLY as appropriate; complete may stay true.
3. Product wire honesty records product residual free (emit SSOT / DualResidual
   agree; no green-wash).
4. Host elaborator residual free is a **separate** program (stays unclaimed).

### Commands (measurement + claim loop)

```bash
just build
just systems-emit-wire   # PRODUCT-RESIDUAL-FREE-MEASURE on release when present
just systems-host        # acceptance + DualResidual free-true pins
just freestanding-self-host-complete  # complete + free living tip; lake when PATH
./src/systems/check.sh
```

## North-star goal

Systems Lean can compile itself to freestanding C with **no classic Lean runtime**
on the **product wire** (the emitted release surface under `emit/` and
`out/freestanding-c` -- not "network wire").

Three claims that must not be conflated:

| Claim | Meaning |
|-------|---------|
| **Product freestanding** | Emitted C has no Lean managed runtime and no product garbage collection (GC) |
| **Slake as compiler** | Real check / lower / emit path for a stated Systems Lean subset -- not readiness canaries alone |
| **Self-application** | That path can process the self-host kernel (and later its own compiler sources) |

Classic Lake remains a valid **bootstrap host** for development. "No classic Lean
runtime" targets the product wire, not "delete Lake tomorrow." Bootstrap S0-S1
use Lake on purpose; S4 retires it only with evidence (see **Bootstrap stages**).

---

## Jargon unpack (this track)

| Term | Meaning here |
|------|----------------|
| **Product wire** | Released freestanding C (and install under `out/freestanding-c`). What consumers link. |
| **Host model** | Lean structural representation of grades / IR / checks. **Not** an AI or machine-learning model. |
| **Direction readiness** | HOST-SELF-HOST V0 inventory canary -- present paths and stage ids only. **Not** self-host. |
| **Kernel** | Smallest freestanding self-host unit set we close first (default: Mult grades). |
| **Closed loop** | Host lowers kernel to IR (+ later host-owned emit) with parity smoke vs product C. |

Canonical terms also live in `doc/vocabulary.md` (**Wire / product wire**,
**Model (host / formal)**).

---

## What does **not** count as self-host

- `HOST-SELF-HOST` / `SLAKE_SELF_HOST_V0` alone (direction canary)
- Retired `SLAKE_COMPILE_PATH_V0` shell stamp alone (deleted; static unit walk pure Nix; host CompilePath is not product C)
- Host Bool readiness maps without IR for a named kernel unit
- Growing bash `EMIT_*` stages as residual theater
- Unlocking `out/llvm-ir` or CompCert PROVABLY from inventory alone

Defaults (open questions answered by plan defaults until human overrides):

1. Mult first.
2. Host `.lean` is the Systems Lean surface for now; `.slake` stays unit-surface markers.
3. Lake bootstrap allowed for development indefinitely.
4. Host-owned freestanding ABI stays compatible with frozen wire contracts where honest.
5. This file is the acceptance home.
6. Full freestanding product kernel rebuilds kernel unlocks llvm **planning**;
   SH5 host-structural `kernelRebuildsKernel` (Mult closed loop + Linear + Types
   + Program + Emit kernel on the classic elaborator) does **not** unlock llvm
   planning as residual-open. Full ladder is progressive after true freestanding
   product self-host.
7. Pure Nix for gates; Lean owns emit truth; thin scripts install only.

- Host elaborator residual (classic Lean managed runtime on Lake) stays separate
  from product wire residual (HOST-DUAL-RESIDUAL canaries + DUAL-RESIDUAL-THEOREM)
- Hosted behavioral probe (`smoke/slake_behavioral_probe.c`) stays smoke debt
  separate from product freestanding wire (`emit/` + `out/freestanding-c/`)
  (HOST-PROBE-WIRE canaries + PROBE-WIRE-THEOREM); probe green does not mean
  residual free

  (HOST-SPEC-PROOF canaries + SPEC-PROOF-THEOREM); Mult..SpecProof readiness
  theorems do not flip proof complete; proofs do not retire tests/smokes
- Do not forge either residual; do not treat probe as product wire; do not
  forge proof complete
